FIELDS = ["open", "high", "low", "close", "prev_close", "volume",
          "turnover", "turnover_rate", "is_paused", "is_st"]


def _date(value):
    return pd.Timestamp(value).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")


def mainboard(symbol):
    return ((symbol.endswith(".SH") and symbol[:3] in ("600", "601", "603", "605")) or
            (symbol.endswith(".SZ") and symbol[:3] in ("000", "001", "002", "003")))


def _decode_models():
    result = {}
    for name, encoded in _EMBEDDED_MODELS.items():
        result[name] = json.loads(zlib.decompress(base64.b64decode(encoded)).decode("utf-8"))
        if result[name]["f"] != MODEL_FEATURES:
            raise ValueError("Embedded model feature mismatch: " + name)
    return result


def _predict_model(model, matrix):
    values = np.asarray(matrix, dtype=float)
    output = np.zeros(values.shape[0], dtype=float)
    for row_index in range(values.shape[0]):
        row = values[row_index]
        total = 0.0
        for nodes in model["t"]:
            node_index = 0
            while True:
                node = nodes[node_index]
                if node[0] == "L":
                    total += float(node[1])
                    break
                feature, threshold, default_left, left, right = node
                value = row[int(feature)]
                go_left = bool(default_left) if not np.isfinite(value) else value <= float(threshold)
                node_index = int(left if go_left else right)
        output[row_index] = total
    return output


def _feature_row(raw, calendar):
    x = raw.copy()
    x.index = pd.DatetimeIndex(x.index).normalize()
    x = x.loc[~x.index.duplicated(keep="last")].sort_index().reindex(calendar)
    if x.empty or pd.isna(x.iloc[-1].close):
        return None
    for column in FIELDS:
        x[column] = pd.to_numeric(x[column], errors="coerce")
    trade = x.is_paused.eq(0)
    r = (x.close / x.prev_close - 1).where(trade)
    chain = (1 + r.fillna(0)).cumprod()
    if len(chain) < 61:
        return None
    values = {}
    for k in (1, 5, 20, 60):
        values["f_ret%s" % k] = chain.iloc[-1] / chain.iloc[-1-k] - 1
    values["f_vol20"] = r.iloc[-20:].std(ddof=1)
    values["f_ma20"] = chain.iloc[-1] / chain.iloc[-20:].mean() - 1
    values["f_amount_ratio5"] = x.turnover.iloc[-1] / x.turnover.iloc[-5:].mean()
    values["f_log_amount"] = np.log1p(x.turnover.iloc[-1])
    values["f_log_price"] = np.log(x.close.iloc[-1])
    values["f_turn"] = x.turnover_rate.iloc[-1]
    values["f_range"] = (x.high.iloc[-1] - x.low.iloc[-1]) / x.prev_close.iloc[-1]
    values["f_downside20"] = np.sqrt(r.clip(upper=0).pow(2).iloc[-20:].mean())
    rr = r.fillna(0)
    prior = chain.shift(1)
    high = chain * (x.high / x.close)
    low = chain * (x.low / x.close)
    true_range = pd.concat([high-low, (high-prior).abs(), (low-prior).abs()], axis=1).max(axis=1) / prior
    atr5 = true_range.iloc[-5:].mean()
    atr20 = true_range.iloc[-20:].mean()
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        values["f_ohlc_atr_compress5_20"] = atr5 / atr20
        values["f_ohlc_rsi14"] = rr.clip(lower=0).iloc[-14:].sum() / rr.abs().iloc[-14:].sum()
    values["observations"] = int(x.close.notna().sum())
    values["prior_close"] = float(x.close.iloc[-1])
    values["prior_amount"] = float(x.turnover.iloc[-1])
    values["st"] = float(x.is_st.iloc[-1])
    return {k: float(np.float32(v)) for k, v in values.items()}


def compute_signals(raw, calendar, models):
    rows = []
    symbols = []
    for symbol in sorted(raw):
        row = _feature_row(raw[symbol], calendar)
        if row is None:
            continue
        if not (row["observations"] >= 120 and np.log(2) <= row["f_log_price"] <= np.log(50)
                and row["f_log_amount"] >= np.log1p(10000000) and row["st"] == 0):
            continue
        if not all(np.isfinite(row[name]) for name in MODEL_FEATURES):
            continue
        rows.append(row)
        symbols.append(symbol)
    if not rows:
        return [], []
    matrix = np.asarray([[row[name] for name in MODEL_FEATURES] for row in rows], dtype=float)
    individual = {name: _predict_model(model, matrix) for name, model in models.items()}
    mean = np.mean(np.vstack([individual["utility"], individual["pen025"], individual["pen100"]]), axis=0)
    ranking = sorted(range(len(symbols)), key=lambda i: (-mean[i], symbols[i]))
    top = []
    for rank, i in enumerate(ranking[:10], 1):
        top.append({"rank": rank, "symbol": symbols[i], "score": float(mean[i]),
                    "prior_close": rows[i]["prior_close"], "prior_amount": rows[i]["prior_amount"]})
    selected = [item for item in top[:TOP_K] if item["score"] >= THRESHOLD]
    return selected, top


def _positions(context):
    p = context.portfolio.positions
    return {s: p[s] for s in list(p.keys()) if p[s].amount > 0}


def _cash(context):
    return float(context.portfolio.stock_account.available_cash)


def _fetch(symbols, count, end=None):
    result = {}
    for begin in range(0, len(symbols), BATCH_SIZE):
        group = symbols[begin:begin+BATCH_SIZE]
        data = history(group, FIELDS, count, "1d", False, None, True, False)
        if not isinstance(data, dict):
            raise TypeError("SuperMind history list call must return dict")
        for symbol in group:
            if symbol not in data or data[symbol] is None or data[symbol].empty:
                continue
            x = data[symbol].copy()
            if any(field not in x.columns for field in FIELDS):
                raise ValueError("Missing history fields: " + symbol)
            x.index = pd.DatetimeIndex(x.index).normalize()
            if end is not None:
                x = x[x.index <= pd.Timestamp(end)]
            result[symbol] = x.loc[~x.index.duplicated(keep="last")].sort_index().iloc[-HISTORY_ROWS:]
    return result


def _valid_bar(bar):
    return (bar is not None and not bar.is_paused and np.isfinite(bar.close) and bar.close > 0 and
            np.isfinite(bar.high_limit) and np.isfinite(bar.low_limit))


def _bar(bars, symbol):
    try:
        return bars[symbol]
    except KeyError:
        return None


def _fee(trade):
    return sum(float(getattr(trade, key, 0) or 0) for key in ("commission", "tax", "transfer_fee"))


def _order_id(result):
    return str(getattr(result, "order_id", result))


def _submit(symbol, quantity, limit, slot, side):
    g.submitting = {"symbol": symbol, "slot": slot, "side": side}
    try:
        result = order(symbol, int(quantity), price=float(limit))
    finally:
        submitting = g.submitting
        g.submitting = None
    if result is None:
        return None
    oid = _order_id(result)
    if oid not in g.orders:
        g.orders[oid] = {"symbol": symbol, "slot": slot, "side": side, "terminal": False}
    if not g.orders[oid]["terminal"]:
        g.symbol_orders[symbol] = oid
    log.info("SCREEN1 SUBMIT side=%s symbol=%s qty=%s limit=%.2f slot=%s id=%s" %
             (side, symbol, quantity, limit, slot, oid))
    return oid


def _free_slots():
    occupied = set(g.symbol_slot.values()) | set(g.reserved_slots)
    return [i for i in range(SLEEVES) if i not in occupied and g.slot_available[i] <= g.session_index]


def _reconcile(context):
    positions = _positions(context)
    active = {str(o.order_id): o for o in (get_open_orders() or [])}
    foreign = [o for o in active.values() if str(o.order_id) not in g.orders]
    if foreign:
        raise RuntimeError("Dedicated account has unmanaged active orders")
    unmanaged = set(positions)-set(g.symbol_slot)-set(g.symbol_orders)
    if unmanaged:
        raise RuntimeError("Dedicated empty-start account required; unmanaged holdings: %s" % sorted(unmanaged))
    for symbol, slot in list(g.symbol_slot.items()):
        if symbol not in positions and symbol not in g.symbol_orders:
            g.symbol_slot.pop(symbol, None)
            g.entry_index.pop(symbol, None)
            g.slot_available[slot] = g.session_index
    return active


def _init(context):
    frequency = str(context.run_info.frequency).lower()
    if frequency not in ("1m", "minute", "minutely"):
        raise ValueError("沪深筛选1平台版要求1分钟频率；初始资金请在平台设为100000元")
    set_benchmark("000905.SH")
    set_commission(PerShare(type="stock", cost=COMMISSION_RATE, min_trade_cost=MIN_COMMISSION))
    set_slippage(PriceSlippage(0.0))
    set_volume_limit(daily=0.01, minute=0.25)
    g.models = _decode_models()
    g.raw = {}
    g.last_signal_day = None
    g.selected = []
    g.top10 = []
    g.session_index = -1
    g.orders = {}
    g.symbol_orders = {}
    g.submitting = None
    g.seen_trades = set()
    g.symbol_slot = {}
    g.entry_index = {}
    g.reserved_slots = set()
    g.slot_cash = [INITIAL_CAPITAL/SLEEVES for _ in range(SLEEVES)]
    g.slot_available = [0 for _ in range(SLEEVES)]
    g.last_minute = None
    if _positions(context):
        raise RuntimeError("沪深筛选1平台版必须使用独立空仓账户")
    log.info("SCREEN1 INIT version=%s model_hashes=%s" % (VERSION, MODEL_HASHES))


def _before_trading(context):
    today = pd.Timestamp(get_datetime()).normalize()
    calendar = pd.DatetimeIndex(get_trade_days(start_date="20180101", end_date=today.strftime("%Y%m%d"))).normalize()
    prior = calendar[calendar < today]
    if len(prior) < 120:
        raise ValueError("回测起点至少需要120个交易日预热")
    g.session_index = len(prior)
    signal_day = prior[-1]
    universe = get_all_securities("stock", date=signal_day.strftime("%Y%m%d"))
    symbols = sorted(set(s for s in universe.index if mainboard(s)) | set(_positions(context)))
    wanted = set(symbols)
    new = sorted(wanted-set(g.raw))
    old = sorted(wanted & set(g.raw))
    if new:
        g.raw.update(_fetch(new, HISTORY_ROWS, signal_day))
    if old:
        for symbol, update in _fetch(old, 8, signal_day).items():
            merged = pd.concat([g.raw[symbol], update])
            g.raw[symbol] = merged.loc[~merged.index.duplicated(keep="last")].sort_index().iloc[-HISTORY_ROWS:]
    g.raw = {symbol: g.raw[symbol] for symbol in symbols if symbol in g.raw}
    feature_calendar = prior[-HISTORY_ROWS:]
    g.selected, g.top10 = compute_signals(g.raw, feature_calendar, g.models)
    g.last_signal_day = _date(signal_day)
    _reconcile(context)
    log.info("SCREEN1 SIGNAL cutoff=%s selected=%s top10=%s" %
             (g.last_signal_day, g.selected, g.top10))


def _buy(context, bars, hhmm):
    if hhmm > BUY_END:
        return
    positions = _positions(context)
    held_or_pending = set(positions) | set(g.symbol_orders)
    for item in g.selected:
        symbol = item["symbol"]
        if symbol in held_or_pending:
            continue
        free = _free_slots()
        if not free:
            return
        bar = _bar(bars, symbol)
        if not _valid_bar(bar) or bar.is_st or bar.open >= bar.high_limit - 1e-8:
            continue
        slot = free[0]
        limit = min(float(bar.high_limit), math.ceil(float(bar.open)*(1+PRICE_TOLERANCE)*100-1e-8)/100)
        budget = min(g.slot_cash[slot], _cash(context))
        quantity = int((budget-MIN_COMMISSION)/(limit*(1+COMMISSION_RATE)+.01)/100)*100
        if quantity <= 0:
            continue
        g.reserved_slots.add(slot)
        if _submit(symbol, quantity, limit, slot, "BUY") is None:
            g.reserved_slots.discard(slot)
        held_or_pending.add(symbol)


def _sell_expired(context, bars, hhmm):
    if hhmm < SELL_START or hhmm > SELL_END:
        return
    for symbol, position in _positions(context).items():
        if symbol in g.symbol_orders or symbol not in g.entry_index:
            continue
        if g.session_index < g.entry_index[symbol] + HOLD_SESSIONS:
            continue
        bar = _bar(bars, symbol)
        if not _valid_bar(bar) or bar.close <= bar.low_limit + 1e-8:
            continue
        quantity = int(position.available_amount)
        if quantity <= 0:
            continue
        limit = max(float(bar.low_limit), math.floor(float(bar.close)*(1-PRICE_TOLERANCE)*100+1e-8)/100)
        _submit(symbol, -quantity, limit, g.symbol_slot[symbol], "SELL")


def _handle_bar(context, bar_dict):
    now = pd.Timestamp(get_datetime())
    stamp = now.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
    if stamp == g.last_minute:
        return
    g.last_minute = stamp
    hhmm = now.hour*100 + now.minute
    if hhmm < 931 or hhmm > SELL_END:
        return
    active = _reconcile(context)
    if hhmm > BUY_END:
        for oid, odr in active.items():
            info = g.orders.get(oid)
            if info is not None and info["side"] == "BUY":
                cancel_order(oid)
    _buy(context, bar_dict, hhmm)
    _sell_expired(context, bar_dict, hhmm)


def _after_trading(context):
    positions = _positions(context)
    log.info("SCREEN1 END date=%s cash=%.2f positions=%s slot_cash=%s" %
             (_date(get_datetime()), _cash(context), sorted(positions), g.slot_cash))


def on_order(context, odr):
    oid = str(odr.order_id)
    info = g.orders.get(oid)
    if info is None and g.submitting is not None and odr.symbol == g.submitting["symbol"]:
        info = dict(g.submitting, terminal=False)
        g.orders[oid] = info
        g.symbol_orders[odr.symbol] = oid
    if info is None:
        return
    status = str(odr.status).split(".")[-1].upper()
    if status in ("FILLED", "REJECTED", "CANCELLED", "CANCELED", "EXPIRED", "EXPIRED_ABSENT"):
        info["terminal"] = True
        symbol, slot = info["symbol"], info["slot"]
        g.symbol_orders.pop(symbol, None)
        if info["side"] == "BUY":
            g.reserved_slots.discard(slot)
            if int(getattr(odr, "filled_amount", 0) or 0) <= 0:
                g.slot_available[slot] = g.session_index + 1
        log.info("SCREEN1 ORDER terminal=%s %s" % (status, odr))


def on_trade(context, trade):
    key = str(getattr(trade, "exec_id", "%s/%s/%s" %
                     (trade.order_id, trade.last_quantity, trade.last_price)))
    if key in g.seen_trades:
        return
    g.seen_trades.add(key)
    oid = str(trade.order_id)
    info = g.orders.get(oid)
    symbol_from_trade = getattr(trade, "order_book_id", getattr(trade, "symbol", None))
    if info is None and g.submitting is not None and symbol_from_trade == g.submitting["symbol"]:
        info = dict(g.submitting, terminal=False)
        g.orders[oid] = info
        g.symbol_orders[symbol_from_trade] = oid
    if info is None:
        return
    symbol, slot = info["symbol"], info["slot"]
    quantity, price = int(trade.last_quantity), float(trade.last_price)
    fee = _fee(trade)
    if info["side"] == "BUY":
        g.slot_cash[slot] -= quantity*price + fee
        if symbol not in g.symbol_slot:
            g.symbol_slot[symbol] = slot
            g.entry_index[symbol] = g.session_index
    else:
        g.slot_cash[slot] += quantity*price - fee
    log.info("SCREEN1 FILL side=%s symbol=%s qty=%s price=%.2f fee=%.2f slot=%s" %
             (info["side"], symbol, quantity, price, fee, slot))


def _run(stage, function, *args):
    try:
        return function(*args)
    except Exception as exc:
        log.info("SCREEN1 FAILED stage=%s error=%s" % (stage, str(exc)))
        raise


def init(context):
    return _run("init", _init, context)


def before_trading(context):
    return _run("before_trading", _before_trading, context)


def handle_bar(context, bar_dict):
    return _run("handle_bar", _handle_bar, context, bar_dict)


def after_trading(context):
    return _run("after_trading", _after_trading, context)
